// FUNC 180020dbf sub_180020DBF
void __fastcall sub_180020DBF(_DWORD *a1, _DWORD *a2)
{
  _DWORD *v2; // rcx
  _DWORD *v3; // rdx

  *a1 = *a2;
  v2 = a1 + 1;
  v3 = a2 + 1;
  *v2 = *v3;
  v2[1] = v3[1];
  JUMPOUT(0x18002063ALL);
}

// FUNC 180020e60 FormatMessageA

        DWORD dwFlags,
        LPCVOID lpSource,

        DWORD dwLanguageId,
        LPSTR lpBuffer,
        DWORD nSize,
